# Changes against witnesses C and H

Counts refer to recorded physical loci, with repeated symbols recorded at their individual places. A locus may differ from both witnesses. Pure macro changes and changes of confidence alone are excluded. High/medium/low count recorded loci, not the probability of an entire page being correct. Resolved doubt marks and retained doubtful cancellations are also documented in the page evidence records.

| 017-B002 | \deleted{\unsure{les} l'opération} | \deleted{les \unsure{l'opération&#124;disjonctions}} | \deleted{\unsure{les} l'opération} | The initial small word has a clear tall l but its e/s body is covered by the heavy strike, so its uncertainty remains. The next phrase begins with a separate tall l and apostrophe, then o, the deep p descender, raised acute stroke, compressed r/a, tall t and dotted i ending in the low on course. These features support l’opération and exclude the proposed disjonctions; the full phrase is crossed by one long independent stroke. Principal audit crop: crops/audit_scan017_017-B002.jpg; original box [570, 205, 1040, 330]. |
| 017-B003 | $X\amalg_Z Y$, \unsure{don&#124;dont} | $X\amalg_Z Y$, des | $X\amalg_Z Y$, \unsure{don&#124;dont} | The coproduct sign has two separate uprights on one long lower bar, with Z below; retain amalgamation notation. After the comma the word shows d with a low bowl, a separate open rounded o and a long flattened n course. No e loop or terminal s turn supports des. A distinct terminal t is not visible, so retain don&#124;dont in that order without supplying the grammatical ending. Principal audit crop: crops/audit_scan017_017-B003.jpg; original box [240, 470, 710, 600]. |
| 017-B004 | $Z\unsure{\mathord{-}&#124;\to}X$ et $Z\mathord{-}Y$ | $Z\to X$ et $Z\to Y$ | $Z\unsure{\mathord{-}&#124;\to}X$ et $Z\mathord{-}Y$ | Both relations are short horizontal shafts with blunt or downturned right caps. The first has a tiny thickened terminal wedge that leaves an arrowhead possible, but no separated upper and lower head strokes; the second ends only in a short downward tick. Retain the doubtful minus/arrow for Z–X and the literal short bar for Z–Y rather than regularize both to complete arrows. Principal audit crop: crops/audit_scan017_017-B004.jpg; original box [745, 425, 1400, 570]. |
| 017-B007 | commute : ces | commute à ces | commute : ces | After commute there are two small separate marks aligned vertically, with no lower bowl or joining stroke. They form a colon, not an accented a. The following compressed ces begins separately at the right; retain commute : ces. Principal audit crop: crops/audit_scan017_017-B007.jpg; original box [240, 735, 835, 865]. |
| 017-B008 | dans un | , dans un | dans un | The crop includes a low comma at its left edge before the two clear words dans un. That comma is already transcribed immediately after opérations in the page; it was outside the first box's selected reading span. The independent comma is therefore additional context, not missing ink in the edition. Retain the first box reading and the existing page punctuation. Principal audit crop: crops/audit_scan017_017-B008.jpg; original box [1155, 695, 1540, 815]. |
| 017-B011 | de t.f., | de t.f\unsure{;&#124;.,} | de t.f., | After de, the crossed tall t has a separate short stop, then a long-descending f with its own detached point to the right. Below and farther right is a distinct long comma. The abbreviation's final stop and following comma are both visible; retain t.f., rather than replacing their two functions with a semicolon. Principal audit crop: crops/audit_scan017_017-B011.jpg; original box [1360, 920, 1660, 1105]. |
| 017-B012 | pourvu, \unsure{(}dans le | pourvu, dans le | pourvu, \unsure{(}dans le | The comma after pourvu is distinct. Immediately before the d bowl of dans there is an additional tall inward-curving stroke with a small retraced lower junction, separate from the d ascender. Its exact form remains crowded, but omitting it loses visible ink; preserve the doubtful opening parenthesis already recorded. The following le has its own tall loop. Principal audit crop: crops/audit_scan017_017-B012.jpg; original box [240, 1160, 825, 1300]. |
| 017-B013 | sommes\unsure{)&#124;,}, qu'on | sommes, \unsure{qu'on&#124;que} | sommes\unsure{)&#124;,}, qu'on | Two separate punctuation strokes follow sommes: a short upper curve close to its ending and a larger low comma farther right. The short curve may be a closing parenthesis or another comma and is not omitted. The following group has q's long descender, u's shoulder, a raised apostrophe-like mark and the flattened on course; qu’on is supported more fully than que. Retain the first reading. Principal audit crop: crops/audit_scan017_017-B013.jpg; original box [1160, 1120, 1655, 1280]. |
